MECHANICAL ASSEMBLY SCRIPT

FILE _scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r5/collect_measurements.py
"""Mechanical inventory/report assembly only; no CAS imports or evaluations."""
import ast
from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shlex
import subprocess

root=Path('/var/projects/toy_physics')
b=root/'_scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r5'
scripts=root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts'
measurement=root/'research/pde_ledger_v3/_measurements/O2_comparator_builder_r5_measurements.txt'
for run in ('tests-01','tests-02','ablations-01','full-01'):
    receipt=json.loads((b/run/'outcome.json').read_text())
    if receipt['exitCode']!=(1 if run=='tests-01' else 0) or not receipt['limitsVerified'] or receipt['childOutcome']['guardReason']:
        raise RuntimeError('run incomplete or operationally unsuccessful: '+run)

catalog=json.loads((b/'catalog.json').read_text())
accounting=[json.loads(line) for line in (b/'accounting.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
s={'tables':{key:len(catalog[key]) for key in ('joins','names','actions','balances')},
   'accounting':dict(Counter(r['accounting'] for r in accounting if 'accounting' in r)),
   'streams':{},'kinds':{},'outcomes':{},'OPEN_occurrence_limits':{},'ablation_map':[]}
for engine in ('py','wl'):
    rows=[r for r in accounting if r.get('kind')=='stream_object_count' and r['engine']==engine]
    s['streams'][engine]={'objects':len(rows),**{key:sum(r[key] for r in rows) for key in
        ('parsed_leaves','accounted_leaves','compared_leaves','unaccounted_leaves')}}
s['generic_unjoined_reasons']=sum(r.get('reason','').startswith('No declared object counterpart') for r in accounting)
kinds,outcomes,verdicts=Counter(),Counter(),Counter()
limits={e:Counter() for e in ('py','wl')}
limit_errors=[]
named_leaks=[]
balance_limits={e:Counter() for e in ('py','wl')}
forbidden={'wl::6','wl::3,8','wl::D','wl::Map'}
scope=None
o4_actions={}
def inventory(result):
    outcomes[result['outcome']]+=1
    for child in result.get('children',{}).values(): inventory(child)
    for child in result.get('operands',[]): inventory(child)
for line in (b/'comparison.jsonl').open():
    row=json.loads(line)
    kinds[row['kind']]+=1
    verdicts.update(re.findall(r'\b(?:PASS|FAIL|AGREE|VERDICT|STATUS)\b',line))
    if row['kind']=='comparison_scope': scope=row['OPEN_limit']
    if row['kind']=='residual': inventory(row['comparison'])
    if row['kind']=='balance_entries':
        for e in ('py','wl'):
            for index,entry in enumerate(row['entries'][e]):
                limit=entry['limit']
                balance_limits[e]['entries']+=1
                balance_limits[e]['outside_leaves']+=limit['outside_inventory_leaves']
                if limit['outside_inventory_leaves']<0 or limit['parsed_leaves']!=limit['inventory_consumed_leaves']+limit['outside_inventory_leaves']:
                    limit_errors.append(['balance',row['row'],e,index])
                if forbidden & entry['inventories']['named_OPEN_operands'].keys():
                    named_leaks.append(['balance',row['row'],e,index])
    if row['kind']=='structure':
        if row['row'].startswith('coupled_embedding'): o4_actions[row['row']]=len(row['action_comparison'])
        for action in row['action_comparison']:
            paired=bool(action['py'] and action['wl'])
            for e in ('py','wl'):
                for local in action['occurrences'][e]:
                    if forbidden & local['inventories']['named_OPEN_operands'].keys():
                        named_leaks.append(['occurrence',row['row'],e,local['local_occurrence']])
                    limit=local['limit']
                    limits[e]['local_occurrences']+=1
                    limits[e]['local_occurrences_on_paired_roles']+=int(paired)
                    limits[e]['occurrences_with_repeated_live_objects']+=bool(limit['repeated_live_objects'])
                    limits[e]['occurrences_with_outside_leaves']+=limit['outside_inventory_leaves']>0
                    if (limit['outside_inventory_leaves']<0 or
                        limit['parsed_leaves']!=limit['inventory_consumed_leaves']+limit['outside_inventory_leaves'] or
                        any(count<=1 for count in limit['repeated_live_objects'].values())):
                        limit_errors.append([row['row'],action['role'],e,local['local_occurrence']])
                    if set(local['inventories'])!={'head','role','orientation','named_OPEN_operands','live_arguments'}:
                        limit_errors.append(['unexpected inventory fields',row['row']])
s.update(kinds=dict(kinds),outcomes=dict(outcomes),OPEN_scope=scope,
         OPEN_occurrence_limits={e:dict(c) for e,c in limits.items()},limit_census_errors=limit_errors,
         literal_verdict_tokens=dict(verdicts),O4_action_counts=o4_actions,
         forbidden_named_inventory_entries=named_leaks,
         balance_limit_totals={e:dict(c) for e,c in balance_limits.items()})
assert not limit_errors and not named_leaks and not verdicts
assert all(v['unaccounted_leaves']==0 for v in s['streams'].values())
ablations=json.loads((b/'ablation-copies/summary.json').read_text())
expected=[f'{prefix}{i}' for prefix,count in (('R',4),('J',4),('P',5),('O',7),('B',4),('L',3),('I',2),('T',1)) for i in range(1,count+1)]
assert [r['bullet'] for r in ablations if not r['bullet'].startswith('extra-')]==expected

def methods(path):
    tree=ast.parse(path.read_text())
    return {n.name:n.lineno for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef)}
test_lines=methods(scripts/'test_O2_cross_engine_comparator.py')
for r in ablations:
    assert r['exit_code']==1 and not r['errors']
    assert all(any(name in failure for failure in r['failing_tests']) for name in r['required_tests'])
    s['ablation_map'].append({'bullet':r['bullet'],'ablation':r['ablation'],
        'required_failing_tests':[{ 'test':test,'line':test_lines[test]} for test in r['required_tests']],
        'all_failures':r['failing_tests'],'resources':r['resources']})
s['test_resources']=(b/'tests-02-resources.txt').read_text().strip()
s['ablation_child_seconds']=json.loads((b/'ablations-01/outcome.json').read_text())['childOutcome']['wallSeconds']
s['ablation_max_peak_rss_kib']=max(int(re.search(r'peak_rss_kib=(\d+)',r['resources'])[1]) for r in ablations)
s['full_resources']=json.loads((b/'full-01/stdout').read_text())
(b/'validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(s,indent=2)+'\n')

with measurement.open('w') as out:
    def write(text): out.write(text+'\n')
    def literal(path):
        write('\nFILE '+str(path.relative_to(root)))
        write(path.read_text())
    write('O2 comparator builder r5 — amendment 1; commands and literal outputs. No residual target supplied.')
    write('BASELINE '+subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=root,text=True).strip())
    write('ASSEMBLY COMMAND python _scratch/s11c/o2-comparator-build-r5/collect_measurements.py')
    write('DELIVERABLE: operand/provenance distinction and consistent leaf counts, balance-entry orientation controls, explicit uncompared wrapper content. No commits or agents.')
    write('\nCOMMAND datalad get research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/out/O2_live_balance_sympy_audit.out research/pde_ledger_v3/mathematica/out/O2_live_balance_mathematica_audit.out')
    literal(b/'datalad-get.log')
    for run in ('tests-01','tests-02','ablations-01','full-01'):
        spec=json.loads((b/run/'invocation.json').read_text())
        command=['python','scripts/s11c_guarded_run.py','--pool','o2-comparator','--memory-gib','6',
                 '--log-directory',str((b/run).relative_to(root)),'--',*spec['command']]
        write('\nCOMMAND '+shlex.join(command))
        for file in ('stdout','stderr','outcome.json','effective-limits.json','duration-validation.json'):
            literal(b/run/file)
    literal(b/'tests-01-resources.txt')
    literal(b/'tests-02-resources.txt')
    write('OWN TEST FIX: tests-01 had two exact leaf-total assertions based on treating association keys as leaves. Corrected expected outside counts 4 to 2 and 3+extra to 2+extra, retaining exact equalities; record keys are node values, not terminal leaves. No production comparison or exclusion assertion weakened.')
    write('\nITEM 6: COMPLETE BULLET / ABLATION / FAILING-TEST MAP')
    write('R=residuals/operands; J=joins/names; P=profiles/derivatives; O=OPEN content; B=balances; L=layout/translation; I=printed limit; T=output. Numbers follow directive order within each category.')
    for r in s['ablation_map']:
        write(json.dumps(r))
    for r in ablations:
        write('\nABLATION '+r['ablation']+' COMMAND '+shlex.join(r['command']))
        for file in ('change.diff','stdout','stderr','resources.txt'):
            literal(b/'ablation-copies'/r['ablation']/file)
    write('\nMECHANICAL OUTPUT INVENTORY (counts and stored fields; no residual interpretation)')
    literal(b/'validation.json')
    write('\nCOMPLETE DECLARED TABLES: both construction-line citations and spec objects on every row')
    literal(b/'catalog.json')
    write('\nCOMPLETE ACCOUNTING: parsed/compared leaves, every unjoined path and reason')
    literal(b/'accounting.jsonl')
    write('\nSHA256 AND BYTES; raw comparison products retained in the new scratch directory')
    paths=[scripts/name for name in ('O2_cross_engine_comparator.py','test_O2_cross_engine_comparator.py','ablate_O2_cross_engine_comparator.py')]
    paths += [scripts/'out/O2_live_balance_sympy_audit.out',scripts.parent/'mathematica/out/O2_live_balance_mathematica_audit.out',b/'comparison.jsonl',b/'accounting.jsonl',b/'catalog.json']
    for path in paths:
        digest=hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open('rb') as stream:
            for chunk in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''): digest.update(chunk)
        write(json.dumps({'path':str(path.relative_to(root)),'sha256':digest.hexdigest(),'bytes':path.stat().st_size}))
    write('\nMECHANICAL ASSEMBLY SCRIPT')
    literal(b/'collect_measurements.py')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in s.items() if k!='ablation_map'},indent=2))
